+import unittest
+from enki.core.react_loop import should_enable_memory_tool, get_active_tools_for_turn
+
+class TestMemoryGating(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_recall_trigger_words(self):
+        """Memastikan kata-kata pemicu recall mengaktifkan tool search_memory."""
+        triggers = [
+            "kemarin kita bahas apa ya?",
+            "pernah nggak kita bicarakan server staging?",
+            "ingat apa yang aku katakan soal database?",
+            "sebelumnya kita sempat diskusikan arsitektur?",
+            "tadi kamu bilang apa?",
+            "waktu itu kuncinya apa ya?",
+            "remember what we discussed earlier?",
+            "earlier you mentioned something about cache"
+        ]
+        for prompt in triggers:
+            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
+                self.assertTrue(
+                    should_enable_memory_tool(prompt),
+                    f"Prompt pemicu recall gagal mengaktifkan memory tool: {prompt}"
+                )
+
+    def test_explicit_memory_keywords(self):
+        """Memastikan kata kunci eksplisit memori mengaktifkan tool search_memory."""
+        explicit_prompts = [
+            "coba cari di catatan masa lalu",
+            "tolong periksa riwayat obrolan kita",
+            "buka log obrolan kemarin",
+            "apa yang ada di sesi lampau?",
+            "cari info di sesi sebelumnya dong",
+            "percakapan lama kita bahas apa?",
+            "cek memori kamu soal topik ini"
+        ]
+        for prompt in explicit_prompts:
+            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
+                self.assertTrue(
+                    should_enable_memory_tool(prompt),
+                    f"Prompt kata kunci eksplisit gagal mengaktifkan memory tool: {prompt}"
+                )
+
+    def test_negative_prompts_do_not_enable_memory_tool(self):
+        """Memastikan prompt umum/coding/resep/sapaan TIDAK mengaktifkan memory tool."""
+        negative_prompts = [
+            "halo selamat pagi enki, gimana kabarmu hari ini?",
+            "jelaskan dong apa itu konsep recursion dalam pemrograman secara ringkas",
+            "siapa pencipta bahasa pemrograman python dan kapan pertama kali dirilis?",
+            "buatkan resep singkat cara bikin nasi goreng kampung yang enak dan gurih",
+            "menurutmu apa perbedaan mendasar antara framework dan library?",
+            "tuliskan contoh fungsi fibonacci rekursif sederhana dalam bahasa python",
+            "bagaimana cara menggunakan docker compose?",
+            "bisa bantu bikin script web scraping?",
+            "coba liat status git kita skrg lg di branch mana",
+            "tolong baca file requirements.txt dong"
+        ]
+        for prompt in negative_prompts:
+            with self.subTest(prompt=prompt):
+                self.assertFalse(
+                    should_enable_memory_tool(prompt),
+                    f"Prompt negatif seharusnya TIDAK mengaktifkan memory tool: {prompt}"
+                )
+
+    def test_get_active_tools_for_turn_schemas(self):
+        """Memastikan tool search_memory benar-benar hilang dari daftar schema saat gating bernilai False."""
+        neg_tools = get_active_tools_for_turn("buatkan resep nasi goreng enak")
+        neg_tool_names = [t["function"]["name"] for t in neg_tools]
+        self.assertNotIn("search_memory", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("read_file", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("git", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("python_version", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("run_unittests", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("list_dir", neg_tool_names)
+        self.assertEqual(len(neg_tools), 5)
+
+        pos_tools = get_active_tools_for_turn("kemarin kita bahas apa ya?")
+        pos_tool_names = [t["function"]["name"] for t in pos_tools]
+        self.assertIn("search_memory", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("read_file", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("git", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("python_version", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("run_unittests", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertIn("list_dir", pos_tool_names)
+        self.assertEqual(len(pos_tools), 6)
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+++ b/tests/test_negative_isolation_failure.py

+import unittest
+import hashlib
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+DATA_DIR = ROOT_DIR / "data"
+
+def get_dir_fingerprint(root_dir: Path) -> dict:
+    fingerprint = {}
+    if not root_dir.exists():
+        return fingerprint
+    for path in sorted(root_dir.rglob("*")):
+        if path.is_file():
+            rel = str(path.relative_to(root_dir)).replace("\\", "/")
+            h = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+            fingerprint[rel] = (path.stat().st_size, h)
+    return fingerprint
+
+class TestNegativeIsolationFailure(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_deliberate_leak_triggers_isolation_failure(self):
+        """Membuktikan bahwa menulis ke data/ asli terdeteksi dan memicu AssertionError pada checker isolasi."""
+        before_data = get_dir_fingerprint(DATA_DIR)
+        leak_file = DATA_DIR / "deliberate_leak_probe.log"
+
+        try:
+            # 1. Sengaja menulis ke data/ asli
+            leak_file.write_text("kebocoran isolasi yang disengaja", encoding="utf-8")
+
+            # 2. Ambil fingerprint setelah kebocoran
+            after_data = get_dir_fingerprint(DATA_DIR)
+            diff_data_added = set(after_data.keys()) - set(before_data.keys())
+
+            # 3. Assert bahwa kebocoran terdeteksi
+            self.assertIn("deliberate_leak_probe.log", diff_data_added)
+
+            # 4. Buktikan bahwa checker isolasi pasti melempar AssertionError jika ada diff
+            with self.assertRaises(AssertionError):
+                self.assertEqual(diff_data_added, set(), f"Kebocoran isolasi terdeteksi di data/: {diff_data_added}")
+        finally:
+            # 5. Bersihkan seketika
+            if leak_file.exists():
+                leak_file.unlink()
+
+        # 6. Pastikan data/ kembali bersih sempurna
+        after_cleanup = get_dir_fingerprint(DATA_DIR)
+        self.assertEqual(before_data, after_cleanup, "Direktori data/ gagal kembali bersih setelah uji negatif.")
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+import unittest
+import tempfile
+import shutil
+from pathlib import Path
+from enki.memory.database import MemoryDatabase
+from enki.memory.recall import execute_passive_recall
+
+class TestPassiveRecall(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+        self.workspace = Path(self.temp_dir).resolve()
+        self.db = MemoryDatabase(self.workspace / "test_passive.db")
+        self.active_session_id = self.db.create_session()
+
+        # Fixture sesi masa lalu di memori
+        old_session = self.db.create_session()
+        self.db.add_turn(
+            old_session,
+            "user",
+            "apa database utama proyek kita ya?"
+        )
+        self.db.add_turn(
+            old_session,
+            "assistant",
+            "Database utama proyek kita adalah SQLite dengan mode WAL dan indeks FTS5."
+        )
+
+        old_session_2 = self.db.create_session()
+        self.db.add_turn(
+            old_session_2,
+            "user",
+            "bahasa apa yg kita pake buat backend service pembayaran?"
+        )
+        self.db.add_turn(
+            old_session_2,
+            "assistant",
+            "Untuk service pembayaran, backend kita menggunakan Python FastAPI."
+        )
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        self.db.close()
+        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
+
+    def test_a_project_database_prompt_triggers_passive_recall(self):
+        """Uji (a): 'apa database utama proyek kita ya?' memunculkan blok recall pasif bila ada turn relevan."""
+        prompt = "apa database utama proyek kita ya?"
+        block = execute_passive_recall(prompt, self.db, self.active_session_id, max_tokens=300)
+
+        print("\n=== RAW OUTPUT UJI (a) PASSIVE RECALL ===")
+        print(block)
+        print("=" * 50)
+
+        self.assertIsNotNone(block, "Blok recall pasif seharusnya muncul untuk pertanyaan database proyek.")
+        self.assertIn("sqlite", block.lower(), "Blok harus memuat cuplikan SQLite dari memori lampau.")
+        self.assertIn("[Konteks Latar dari Percakapan Lama]", block, "Header harus bertuliskan konteks latar pasif.")
+        self.assertNotIn("[Instruksi Wajib]: Jawab berdasarkan hasil pencarian di atas", block, "Recall pasif TIDAK boleh memiliki grounding keras.")
+
+    def test_b_generic_prompts_do_not_trigger_passive_recall(self):
+        """Uji (b): Pesan umum ('siapa pencipta python', 'resep nasi goreng') TIDAK memunculkan blok
+        walaupun database memori menyimpan turn tentang backend/Python.
+        """
+        generic_prompts = [
+            "siapa pencipta bahasa pemrograman python dan kapan pertama kali dirilis?",
+            "buatkan resep singkat cara bikin nasi goreng kampung yang enak dan gurih",
+            "halo selamat pagi enki, gimana kabarmu hari ini?",
+            "apa beda framework dan library dalam pemrograman web?"
+        ]
+
+        print("\n=== RAW OUTPUT UJI (b) PASSIVE RECALL (HARUS NONE) ===")
+        for p in generic_prompts:
+            block = execute_passive_recall(p, self.db, self.active_session_id, max_tokens=300)
+            print(f"Prompt: '{p}' -> Result: {block}")
+            self.assertIsNone(
+                block,
+                f"Prompt umum '{p}' seharusnya TIDAK memicu recall pasif!"
+            )
+        print("=" * 50)
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+import unittest
+import tempfile
+import shutil
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional
+
+from enki.core.react_loop import ReActCoordinator
+from enki.core.prompt_assembler import PromptAssembler, DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT
+from enki.memory.database import MemoryDatabase
+
+class MockOllamaClient:
+    """Mock client untuk menguji alur ReAct loop tanpa LLM."""
+    def __init__(self, step_responses: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
+        self.step_responses = step_responses
+        self.call_history = []
+        self.call_index = 0
+
+    def chat(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, Any]], tools: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None, **kwargs) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+        self.call_history.append({
+            "model": model,
+            "messages_count": len(messages),
+            "tools": tools,
+            "has_tools": tools is not None
+        })
+        if self.call_index < len(self.step_responses):
+            resp = self.step_responses[self.call_index]
+            self.call_index += 1
+            return resp
+        # Default response if out of steps
+        return {"content": "Jawaban default selesai.", "tool_calls": None}
+
+    def chat_stream(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, Any]], **kwargs):
+        yield "Jawaban fallback stream selesai.", {"prompt_eval_count": 10, "eval_count": 5}
+
+class TestPolicyRejectionHandling(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+        self.workspace = Path(self.temp_dir).resolve()
+        self.db = MemoryDatabase(self.workspace / "test.db")
+        self.session_id = self.db.create_session()
+        self.assembler = PromptAssembler(system_prompt=DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT, profile="")
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        self.db.close()
+        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
+
+    def test_policy_rejection_sends_next_iteration_without_tools(self):
+        """Memastikan penolakan kebijakan (path traversal di luar workspace) menyebabkan
+        request berikutnya dikirim TANPA tools (tools=None) dan menghasilkan teks.
+        """
+        step1_response = {
+            "content": "",
+            "tool_calls": [{
+                "function": {
+                    "name": "read_file",
+                    "arguments": json.dumps({"file_path": "../outside.txt"})
+                }
+            }]
+        }
+        step2_response = {
+            "content": "Maaf, akses ke luar workspace ditolak.",
+            "tool_calls": None
+        }
+
+        mock_client = MockOllamaClient([step1_response, step2_response])
+        coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+            client=mock_client,
+            assembler=self.assembler,
+            workspace_root=self.workspace,
+            db=self.db,
+            max_iterations=5
+        )
+
+        chunks = []
+        for chunk, _ in coordinator.run_react_turn("baca file di luar", self.session_id, history=[]):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+
+        ans = "".join(chunks)
+        self.assertEqual(ans, "Maaf, akses ke luar workspace ditolak.")
+        self.assertEqual(len(mock_client.call_history), 2)
+
+        # Iterasi 1: Memiliki tools aktif
+        self.assertTrue(mock_client.call_history[0]["has_tools"])
+        # Iterasi 2: Dikirim TANPA tools (tools=None)
+        self.assertFalse(mock_client.call_history[1]["has_tools"])
+        self.assertIsNone(mock_client.call_history[1]["tools"])
+
+    def test_unregistered_tool_triggers_policy_rejection_without_tools(self):
+        """Memastikan tool yang tidak terdaftar memicu penolakan kebijakan dan iterasi berikutnya tanpa tools."""
+        step1_response = {
+            "content": "",
+            "tool_calls": [{
+                "function": {
+                    "name": "run_shell_command",  # Tool yang sudah dihapus
+                    "arguments": json.dumps({"command": "dir"})
+                }
+            }]
+        }
+        step2_response = {
+            "content": "Tool run_shell_command tidak diizinkan lagi.",
+            "tool_calls": None
+        }
+
+        mock_client = MockOllamaClient([step1_response, step2_response])
+        coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+            client=mock_client,
+            assembler=self.assembler,
+            workspace_root=self.workspace,
+            db=self.db
+        )
+
+        chunks = []
+        for chunk, _ in coordinator.run_react_turn("jalankan dir", self.session_id, history=[]):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+
+        ans = "".join(chunks)
+        self.assertEqual(ans, "Tool run_shell_command tidak diizinkan lagi.")
+        self.assertEqual(len(mock_client.call_history), 2)
+        self.assertFalse(mock_client.call_history[1]["has_tools"])
+
+    def test_invalid_argument_allows_one_retry_then_disables_tools(self):
+        """Memastikan invalid argumen boleh diulang 1 kali, tetapi kegagalan argumen kedua
+        langsung mematikan tools (tools=None).
+        """
+        # Step 1: Argumen salah pertama (missing action)
+        step1_response = {
+            "content": "",
+            "tool_calls": [{
+                "function": {
+                    "name": "git",
+                    "arguments": "{}"
+                }
+            }]
+        }
+        # Step 2: Retry pertama (masih diperbolehkan tools), tapi LLM masih mengirim argumen salah
+        step2_response = {
+            "content": "",
+            "tool_calls": [{
+                "function": {
+                    "name": "git",
+                    "arguments": "{}"
+                }
+            }]
+        }
+        # Step 3: Karena retry habis, step 3 WAJIB dipanggil dengan tools=None
+        step3_response = {
+            "content": "Gagal menjalankan git karena aksi tidak ditentukan.",
+            "tool_calls": None
+        }
+
+        mock_client = MockOllamaClient([step1_response, step2_response, step3_response])
+        coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+            client=mock_client,
+            assembler=self.assembler,
+            workspace_root=self.workspace,
+            db=self.db,
+            max_iterations=5
+        )
+
+        chunks = []
+        for chunk, _ in coordinator.run_react_turn("cek git", self.session_id, history=[]):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+
+        ans = "".join(chunks)
+        self.assertEqual(ans, "Gagal menjalankan git karena aksi tidak ditentukan.")
+        self.assertEqual(len(mock_client.call_history), 3)
+
+        # Call 1: Ada tools
+        self.assertTrue(mock_client.call_history[0]["has_tools"])
+        # Call 2: Retry pertama diperbolehkan ada tools
+        self.assertTrue(mock_client.call_history[1]["has_tools"])
+        # Call 3: Retry habis -> WAJIB tanpa tools
+        self.assertFalse(mock_client.call_history[2]["has_tools"])
+        self.assertIsNone(mock_client.call_history[2]["tools"])
+
+    def test_reading_file_with_rejection_string_does_not_trigger_policy_rejection(self):
+        """Membaca file sah yang teksnya memuat '[Akses Ditolak]' TIDAK BOLEH memicu policy rejection di ReAct loop."""
+        test_file = self.workspace / "faq.txt"
+        test_file.write_text("[Akses Ditolak] terjadi saat permission 000.", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        step1_response = {
+            "content": "",
+            "tool_calls": [{
+                "function": {
+                    "name": "read_file",
+                    "arguments": json.dumps({"file_path": "faq.txt"})
+                }
+            }]
+        }
+        step2_response = {
+            "content": "Isi FAQ menjelaskan tentang [Akses Ditolak].",
+            "tool_calls": None
+        }
+
+        mock_client = MockOllamaClient([step1_response, step2_response])
+        coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+            client=mock_client,
+            assembler=self.assembler,
+            workspace_root=self.workspace,
+            db=self.db,
+            max_iterations=5
+        )
+
+        chunks = []
+        for chunk, _ in coordinator.run_react_turn("baca faq.txt", self.session_id, history=[]):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+
+        ans = "".join(chunks)
+        self.assertEqual(ans, "Isi FAQ menjelaskan tentang [Akses Ditolak].")
+        self.assertEqual(len(mock_client.call_history), 2)
+        # Call 1: Ada tools
+        self.assertTrue(mock_client.call_history[0]["has_tools"])
+        # Call 2: Karena pembacaan file berhasil (bukan policy rejection), tools TIDAK dicabut!
+        self.assertTrue(mock_client.call_history[1]["has_tools"])
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    import json
+    unittest.main()

+    def test_code_level_injection_allowlist_command_aborts_without_subprocess(self):
+        """Tes tingkat kode TANPA LLM:
+        Konten memuat instruksi untuk perintah git log.
+        Assert fungsi konfirmasi dipanggil dengan perintah persis ('git log -n 5'),
+        dan kalau konfirmasi menolak, subprocess.run (di-mock) TIDAK PERNAH dipanggil.

+            return False  # Menolak eksekusi [y/N]
+

+            ok, is_policy_rejection, result = execute_git_command(
+                action="log",
+                workspace_root=self.workspace_root,
+                confirm_func=mock_confirm_reject,
+                audit_log_path=self.data_dir / "tool_audit.log"
+            )
+
+            # 1. Konfirmasi dipanggil dengan perintah persis
+            self.assertEqual(confirmed_commands, ["git log -n 5"])
+            # 2. Hasil eksekusi menyatakan dibatalkan dan policy rejection
+            self.assertTrue(is_policy_rejection)
+            self.assertIn("dibatalkan oleh pengguna", result)
+            # 3. Subprocess TIDAK PERNAH dipanggil
+            mock_subprocess.assert_not_called()

+def setup_benchmark_fixtures_set3(workspace: Path, db: MemoryDatabase):

+    (workspace / "licenses.txt").write_text("Daftar Lisensi Proyek:\n1. MIT License\n2. Apache-2.0 License", encoding="utf-8")
+
+    # 2. Database past session fixtures (Bias vs Recall Truth)

+
+    s2 = db.create_session()
+    db.add_turn(s2, "user", "bahasa apa yg kita pake buat backend service pembayaran?")
+    db.add_turn(s2, "assistant", "Untuk service pembayaran, backend kita menggunakan Python FastAPI.")
+
+    s3 = db.create_session()

+    db.add_turn(s3, "assistant", "Server staging kita disepakati berjalan pada port 9042.")
+
+    s4 = db.create_session()

+    db.add_turn(s4, "assistant", "Lead engineer proyek ENKI adalah Budi Santoso.")

+    prompt_item: Dict[str, Any],
+    coordinator: ReActCoordinator,
+    session_id: str,
+    model: str = "qwen2.5:3b"
+) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Menjalankan 1 prompt dalam ReAct loop multi-turn untuk Set 3."""
+    user_prompt = prompt_item["prompt"]
+    expected_tool = prompt_item["expected_tool"]
+    expected_arg = prompt_item["expected_arg"]
+    unique_token = prompt_item["unique_token"]
+
+    tool_calls_recorded = []
+    tool_observations_recorded = []
+
+    def trace_cb(evt: str, msg: str):
+        if evt == "tool_call":
+            tool_calls_recorded.append(msg)
+        elif evt == "tool_result":
+            tool_observations_recorded.append(msg)
+
+    orig_execute_tool = coordinator.execute_tool
+    def instrumented_execute_tool(tool_name: str, args: Dict[str, Any], session_id: str = "") -> str:
+        if tool_name == "run_shell_command":
+            cmd = args.get("command", "")
+            return mock_shell_output_set3(cmd)
+        return orig_execute_tool(tool_name, args, session_id=session_id)
+
+    coordinator.execute_tool = instrumented_execute_tool
+
+    chunks = []
+    final_metrics = {}
+    is_timeout = False
+    try:
+        for chunk, metrics in coordinator.run_react_turn(
+            user_input=user_prompt,
+            session_id=session_id,
+            history=[],
+            model=model,
+            num_ctx=8192,
+            temperature=0.2,
+            trace_callback=trace_cb
+        ):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+            if metrics:
+                final_metrics = metrics
+    except Exception as e:
+        if "timed out" in str(e).lower() or "timeout" in str(e).lower():
+            is_timeout = True
+    finally:
+        coordinator.execute_tool = orig_execute_tool
+
+    final_text = "".join(chunks).strip()
+
+    first_tool_call = None
+    first_tool_arg = None
+    if tool_calls_recorded:
+        raw_call = tool_calls_recorded[0]
+        paren_idx = raw_call.find("(")
+        if paren_idx != -1:
+            first_tool_call = raw_call[:paren_idx].strip()
+            first_tool_arg = raw_call[paren_idx + 1 : -1].strip()
+
+    # Evaluasi
+    is_false_call = False
+    is_wrong_tool = False
+    is_invalid_arg = False
+    is_fidelity_ok = False
+    is_success = False
+    sandbox_status = "NONE"
+
+    if expected_tool is None:
+        # Negatif
+        if first_tool_call is not None:
+            is_false_call = True
+            sandbox_status = "BLOCKED" if "blocked" in "".join(tool_observations_recorded).lower() else "ATTEMPTED"
+        else:
+            is_success = True
+            is_fidelity_ok = True
+    else:
+        # Positif
+        if first_tool_call is None:
+            is_wrong_tool = True
+        elif first_tool_call != expected_tool:
+            is_wrong_tool = True
+        else:
+            if expected_arg:
+                arg_matched = expected_arg.lower() in (first_tool_arg or "").lower()
+                if not arg_matched:
+                    is_invalid_arg = True
+                else:
+                    if unique_token:
+                        if unique_token.lower() in final_text.lower():
+                            is_fidelity_ok = True
+                            is_success = True
+                        else:
+                            is_fidelity_ok = False
+                    else:
+                        is_fidelity_ok = True
+                        is_success = True
+            else:
+                is_fidelity_ok = True
+                is_success = True
+
+    routing_ok = (not is_false_call) and (not is_wrong_tool)
+    valid_args = not is_invalid_arg
+
+    return {
+        "id": prompt_item["id"],
+        "prompt_input": user_prompt,
+        "tool_calls": tool_calls_recorded,
+        "called_tool": first_tool_call,
+        "called_arg": first_tool_arg,
+        "tool_observations": tool_observations_recorded,
+        "final_response": final_text,
+        "evaluation": {
+            "routing_ok": routing_ok,
+            "valid_args": valid_args,
+            "fidelity_ok": is_fidelity_ok,
+            "sandbox_status": sandbox_status,
+            "is_false_call": is_false_call,
+            "is_wrong_tool": is_wrong_tool,
+            "is_invalid_arg": is_invalid_arg,
+            "is_success": is_success,
+            "is_timeout": is_timeout
+        },
+        "metrics": final_metrics,
+        "is_false_call": is_false_call,
+        "is_wrong_tool": is_wrong_tool,
+        "is_invalid_arg": is_invalid_arg,
+        "is_fidelity_ok": is_fidelity_ok,
+        "is_success": is_success,
+        "is_timeout": is_timeout,
+        "final_text_preview": final_text[:120].replace("\n", " ")
+    }
+
+class TestReActBenchmarkSet3(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+        self.workspace_root = Path(self.temp_dir)
+        self.db_path = self.workspace_root / "bench_sessions_s3.db"
+        self.db = MemoryDatabase(db_path=self.db_path)
+        self.session_id = self.db.create_session()
+        os.environ["ENKI_DATA_DIR"] = str(self.workspace_root / "data")
+        os.environ["ENKI_LOG_DIR"] = str(self.workspace_root / "logs")
+        setup_benchmark_fixtures_set3(self.workspace_root, self.db)
+        self.client = OllamaClient()
+        self.assembler = PromptAssembler(system_prompt=DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT, profile="")
+        self.coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+            client=self.client,
+            assembler=self.assembler,
+            workspace_root=self.workspace_root,
+            db=self.db,
+            confirm_func=lambda _: True,
+            audit_log_path=self.workspace_root / "data" / "tool_audit.log"
+        )
+
+    def tearDown(self):
+        self.db.close()
+        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
+        if "ENKI_DATA_DIR" in os.environ:
+            del os.environ["ENKI_DATA_DIR"]
+        if "ENKI_LOG_DIR" in os.environ:
+            del os.environ["ENKI_LOG_DIR"]
+
+    @unittest.skip("Benchmark suite - run explicitly via python tests/test_react_benchmark_set3.py")
+    def test_run_3_iterations_benchmark_set3(self):
+        """Menjalankan 24 prompt multi-turn UJIAN (Set 3) sebanyak 3 iterasi."""
+        if not self.client.is_ready():
+            self.skipTest("Ollama server tidak aktif")
+
+        results_dir = ROOT_DIR / "tests" / "results"
+        results_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+        jsonl_path = results_dir / "benchmark_set3_raw.jsonl"
+        if jsonl_path.exists():
+            jsonl_path.unlink()
+
+        print("\n" + "=" * 75)
+        print("KRITERIA KELULUSAN BENCHMARK SET 3 (UJIAN):")
+        print("  1. Routing Positif     : >= 90.0%")
+        print("  2. False Call Negatif  : <= 10.0% (prompt konfirmasi dilaporkan terpisah)")
+        print("  3. Fidelity            : >= 85.0%")
+        print("  4. Timeout             : 0")
+        print("  5. Invalid Argumen     : 0")
+        print("=" * 75)
+        print("MEMULAI BENCHMARK SET KETIGA (24 PROMPT x 3 RUNS - MULTI-TURN REACT)")
+        print("=" * 75)
+
+        runs_results = []
+        num_runs = 3
+
+        for run_idx in range(1, num_runs + 1):
+            t_run_start = time.time()
+            print(f"\n--- MENJALANKAN RUN {run_idx}/{num_runs} ---")
+            run_items = []
+            for item in BENCHMARK_PROMPTS_SET3:
+                res = run_single_benchmark_turn_set3(
+                    prompt_item=item,
+                    coordinator=self.coordinator,
+                    session_id=self.session_id,
+                    model="qwen2.5:3b"
+                )
+                run_items.append(res)
+
+                jsonl_record = {
+                    "benchmark_set": "set_3_exam",
+                    "run_idx": run_idx,
+                    "id": res["id"],
+                    "prompt_input": res["prompt_input"],
+                    "tool_calls": res["tool_calls"],
+                    "tool_observations": res["tool_observations"],
+                    "final_response": res["final_response"],
+                    "evaluation": res["evaluation"],
+                    "metrics": res["metrics"]
+                }
+                with open(jsonl_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                    f.write(json.dumps(jsonl_record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
+
+                status_str = "OK" if res["is_success"] else "FAIL"
+                print(f"[{res['id']}] {status_str} | Tool: {res['called_tool']} | Success: {res['is_success']}")
+
+            dur = round(time.time() - t_run_start, 2)
+            print(f"Run {run_idx} selesai dalam {dur} detik.")
+            runs_results.append(run_items)
+
+        # Analisis metrik
+        total_tests = len(BENCHMARK_PROMPTS_SET3) * num_runs
+        pos_tests = 12 * num_runs
+        neg_tests = 12 * num_runs
+
+        pos_routing_success = 0
+        neg_false_calls = 0
+        pos_fidelity_pass = 0
+        total_timeouts = 0
+        total_invalid_args = 0
+        shell_prompt_attempts = 0
+
+        print("\n" + "=" * 75)
+        print("HASIL MENTAH PER PROMPT SET 3 (3 RUNS):")
+        print(f"{'ID':<14} | {'Expected Tool':<18} | {'Run 1':<6} | {'Run 2':<6} | {'Run 3':<6} | {'Status Agregat'}")
+        print("-" * 75)
+
+        for p_idx, prompt_item in enumerate(BENCHMARK_PROMPTS_SET3):
+            p_id = prompt_item["id"]
+            exp_tool = str(prompt_item["expected_tool"])
+            r1 = "PASS" if runs_results[0][p_idx]["is_success"] else "FAIL"
+            r2 = "PASS" if runs_results[1][p_idx]["is_success"] else "FAIL"
+            r3 = "PASS" if runs_results[2][p_idx]["is_success"] else "FAIL"
+
+            passes = [runs_results[r][p_idx]["is_success"] for r in range(num_runs)]
+            agg = f"{sum(passes)}/{num_runs}"
+            print(f"{p_id:<14} | {exp_tool:<18} | {r1:<6} | {r2:<6} | {r3:<6} | {agg}")
+
+            is_positive = prompt_item["expected_tool"] is not None
+
+            for r in range(num_runs):
+                item_res = runs_results[r][p_idx]
+                if is_positive:
+                    if not item_res["is_wrong_tool"]:
+                        pos_routing_success += 1
+                    if item_res["is_fidelity_ok"]:
+                        pos_fidelity_pass += 1
+                else:
+                    if item_res["is_false_call"]:
+                        neg_false_calls += 1
+                        if item_res["called_tool"] == "run_shell_command":
+                            shell_prompt_attempts += 1
+
+                if item_res["is_invalid_arg"]:
+                    total_invalid_args += 1
+                if item_res["is_timeout"]:
+                    total_timeouts += 1
+
+        routing_pct = (pos_routing_success / pos_tests) * 100
+        false_call_pct = (neg_false_calls / neg_tests) * 100
+        fidelity_pct = (pos_fidelity_pass / pos_tests) * 100
+
+        print("=" * 75)
+        print("RINGKASAN METRIK AKHIR BENCHMARK SET 3 (UJIAN):")
+        print(f"Total Uji (24x3)             : {total_tests}")
+        print(f"Routing Positif (>=90%)      : {pos_routing_success}/{pos_tests} ({routing_pct:.1f}%)")
+        print(f"False Call Negatif (<=10%)   : {neg_false_calls}/{neg_tests} ({false_call_pct:.1f}%)")
+        print(f"  - Shell Confirmation Prompt: {shell_prompt_attempts} kali dipicu oleh model")
+        print(f"Fidelity Jawaban (>=85%)     : {pos_fidelity_pass}/{pos_tests} ({fidelity_pct:.1f}%)")
+        print(f"Total Timeout (=0)           : {total_timeouts}")
+        print(f"Invalid Argumen (=0)         : {total_invalid_args}")
+        print(f"Raw JSONL tersimpan di       : {jsonl_path}")
+        print("=" * 75)
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    unittest.main()

+LOCK_FILE = ROOT_DIR / "tests" / ".benchmark_lock"
+
+class BenchmarkLock:
+    """Kunci file agar dua benchmark tidak bisa dijalankan bersamaan."""
+    def __enter__(self):
+        if LOCK_FILE.exists():
+            try:
+                content = LOCK_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
+                lock_data = json.loads(content)
+                pid = lock_data.get("pid")
+                t_str = lock_data.get("time_str", "waktu tidak diketahui")
+                raise RuntimeError(
+                    f"\n[BENCHMARK LOCK ERROR] Benchmark terkunci oleh proses PID={pid} pada {t_str}.\n"
+                    f"File lock: {LOCK_FILE}\n"
+                    f"Eksekusi dibatalkan untuk mencegah tabrakan/konkurensi."
+                )
+            except json.JSONDecodeError:
+                raise RuntimeError(f"\n[BENCHMARK LOCK ERROR] File lock {LOCK_FILE} ditemukan. Ada benchmark lain yang sedang berjalan.")
+
+        lock_info = {
+            "pid": os.getpid(),
+            "timestamp": time.time(),
+            "time_str": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
+        }
+        LOCK_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(lock_info, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+        return self
+
+    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
+        if LOCK_FILE.exists():
+            try:
+                LOCK_FILE.unlink()
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+
+def check_ollama_idle(client: OllamaClient):
+    """Memeriksa kesiapan server Ollama dan status model aktif di VRAM."""
+    if not client.is_ready():
+        raise RuntimeError("Server Ollama tidak aktif di http://127.0.0.1:11434!")
+
+    try:
+        proc = subprocess.run(["ollama", "ps"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
+        if proc.returncode == 0:
+            lines = [l.strip() for l in proc.stdout.strip().splitlines() if l.strip()]
+            print("\n[PREFLIGHT CHECK] Status 'ollama ps':")
+            if len(lines) <= 1:
+                print("  Ollama VRAM: Idle / Kosong (tidak ada model yang sedang berjalan).")
+            else:
+                for line in lines:
+                    print(f"  {line}")
+        else:
+            print("[PREFLIGHT CHECK] Gagal menjalankan 'ollama ps' (returncode non-zero).")
+    except Exception as e:
+        print(f"[PREFLIGHT CHECK] Tidak dapat menjalankan 'ollama ps': {e}")
+

+        "expected_arg": "requirements.txt",
+        "expected_key": "fastapi"
+    },
+    {
+        "id": "S4R_P08_REG",
+        "origin_id": "S3_P09_GIT",
+        "prompt": "bisa tolong liat branch git kita yg aktif dan status commit terakhir?",
+        "expected_tool": "git",
+        "expected_arg": "status",
+        "expected_key": "feat/enki-core-v2"
+    },
+    {
+        "id": "S4R_P09_REG",
+        "origin_id": "S3_P11_TEST",
+        "prompt": "tolong jalanin unit test proyek kita dong",
+        "expected_tool": "run_unittests",
+        "expected_arg": None,
+        "expected_key": "ok"
+    },
+    {
+        "id": "S4R_P10_REG",
+        "origin_id": "S3_P14_NEG",
+        "prompt": "tolong jelaskan arsitektur Transformer dan mekanisme self-attention",
+        "expected_tool": None,
+        "notes": "Uji no-tool untuk topik ML teoritis"
+    }
+]
+
+def load_user_prompts(prompts_path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+    """Membaca dan memvalidasi file prompt dari pengguna.
+    Wajib minimal 15 baris valid, atau melempar RuntimeError.
+    """
+    if not prompts_path.exists():
+        raise RuntimeError(f"File prompt user tidak ditemukan: {prompts_path}")
+
+    valid_prompts = []
+    lines = prompts_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+    user_idx = 1
+
+    for line in lines:
+        line_clean = line.strip()
+        if not line_clean or line_clean.startswith("#"):
+            continue
+
+        parts = [p.strip() for p in line_clean.split("|")]
+        p_type = parts[0].upper()
+
+        if p_type == "MEM":
+            if len(parts) < 4:
+                continue
+            prompt_text = parts[1]
+            mem_sentence = parts[2]
+            key_val = parts[3]
+            valid_prompts.append({
+                "id": f"S4A_U{user_idx:02d}_MEM",
+                "category": "MEM",
+                "prompt": prompt_text,
+                "expected_tool": None,
+                "expected_key": key_val,
+                "memory_inject": ("user", prompt_text, "assistant", mem_sentence)
+            })
+            user_idx += 1
+
+        elif p_type in ("FILE", "GIT", "TEST", "NEG"):
+            if len(parts) < 2:
+                continue
+            prompt_text = parts[1]
+            exp_tool = None
+            exp_arg = None
+            if p_type == "FILE":
+                exp_tool = "read_file"
+                exp_arg = "requirements.txt" if "requirements" in prompt_text.lower() else "README.md"
+            elif p_type == "GIT":
+                exp_tool = "git"
+                exp_arg = "diff" if "diff" in prompt_text.lower() else "status"
+            elif p_type == "TEST":
+                exp_tool = "run_unittests"
+
+            valid_prompts.append({
+                "id": f"S4A_U{user_idx:02d}_{p_type}",
+                "category": p_type,
+                "prompt": prompt_text,
+                "expected_tool": exp_tool,
+                "expected_arg": exp_arg,
+                "expected_key": None
+            })
+            user_idx += 1
+
+    if len(valid_prompts) < 15:
+        sys.stderr.write(
+            f"\n[FATAL RUNNER ERROR]: {prompts_path} hanya berisi {len(valid_prompts)} prompt valid "
+            f"(diperlukan minimal 15 baris: 6 MEM, 4 WS, 5 NEG).\n"
+            f"Runner Set 4 GAGAL KERAS dan MENOLAK BERJALAN.\n"
+        )
+        sys.exit(1)
+
+    return valid_prompts
+
+def setup_real_temporary_workspace(root_dir: Path):
+    """Membuat direktori kerja nyata dengan git repo aktif, commit, uncommitted change, dan unittest."""
+    root_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+    # 1. README.md
+    (root_dir / "README.md").write_text("# ENKI Project\nAsisten AI lokal untuk developer pada GPU 4GB.", encoding="utf-8")
+
+    # 2. requirements.txt
+    (root_dir / "requirements.txt").write_text("fastapi==0.110.0\nuvicorn==0.28.0\npydantic==2.6.4\npytest==8.1.1\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+    # 3. app/main.py
+    app_dir = root_dir / "app"
+    app_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    (app_dir / "main.py").write_text("def get_status():\n    return 'OK'\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+    # 4. tests/test_app.py
+    test_dir = root_dir / "tests"
+    test_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    (test_dir / "test_app.py").write_text(
+        "import unittest\nfrom app.main import get_status\n\n"
+        "class TestApp(unittest.TestCase):\n"
+        "    def test_status(self):\n        self.assertEqual(get_status(), 'OK')\n"
+        "    def test_health(self):\n        self.assertTrue(True)\n\n"
+        "if __name__ == '__main__':\n    unittest.main()\n",
+        encoding="utf-8"
+    )
+
+    # 5. Inisialisasi Git nyata
+    subprocess.run(["git", "init"], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+    subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "ENKI Tester"], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+    subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "tester@enki.internal"], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+    subprocess.run(["git", "checkout", "-b", "feat/enki-core-v2"], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+    subprocess.run(["git", "add", "."], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+    subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-m", "feat: initial commit"], cwd=str(root_dir), stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, check=True)
+
+    # 6. Buat perubahan belum dicommit agar git diff dan git status nyata
+    (app_dir / "main.py").write_text("def get_status():\n    return 'OK'\n\n# uncommitted change for benchmark test\n", encoding="utf-8")
+
+def run_single_benchmark_turn(
+    prompt_item: Dict[str, Any],
+    coordinator: ReActCoordinator,
+    session_id: str,
+    db: MemoryDatabase,
+    confirmed_commands_log: List[str],
+    model: str = "qwen2.5:3b"
+) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Menjalankan 1 prompt dalam benchmark Set 4 dengan pencatatan metrik detail."""
+    user_prompt = prompt_item["prompt"]
+    expected_tool = prompt_item.get("expected_tool")
+    expected_arg = prompt_item.get("expected_arg")
+    expected_key = prompt_item.get("expected_key")
+    category = prompt_item.get("category", "NEG")
+
+    tool_calls_recorded = []
+    tool_observations_recorded = []
+    timeout_reason = "none"
+
+    def trace_cb(evt: str, msg: str):
+        if evt == "tool_call":
+            tool_calls_recorded.append(msg)
+        elif evt == "tool_result":
+            tool_observations_recorded.append(msg)
+        elif evt == "timeout":
+            nonlocal timeout_reason
+            timeout_reason = "turn_timeout"
+
+    # Evaluasi passive recall deterministik di muka
+    recall_injected = False
+    recall_content = execute_passive_recall(user_prompt, db, session_id, max_tokens=300, df_threshold_pct=5.0)
+    if recall_content:
+        recall_injected = True
+
+    chunks = []
+    final_metrics = {}
+    is_timeout = False
+
+    try:
+        for chunk, metrics in coordinator.run_react_turn(
+            user_input=user_prompt,
+            session_id=session_id,
+            history=[],
+            model=model,
+            recall_content=recall_content,
+            trace_callback=trace_cb
+        ):
+            if chunk:
+                chunks.append(chunk)
+            if metrics:
+                final_metrics = metrics
+    except TimeoutError as te:
+        is_timeout = True
+        timeout_reason = "no_first_token" if len(chunks) == 0 else "http_request_timeout"
+    except Exception as e:
+        if "timeout" in str(e).lower() or "timed out" in str(e).lower():
+            is_timeout = True
+            timeout_reason = "no_first_token" if len(chunks) == 0 else "http_request_timeout"
+
+    if is_timeout and len(chunks) == 0:
+        timeout_reason = "no_first_token"
+    elif timeout_reason == "turn_timeout" and len(chunks) == 0:
+        is_timeout = True
+        timeout_reason = "no_first_token"
+
+    final_text = "".join(chunks).strip()
+
+    first_tool_call = None
+    first_tool_arg = None
+    if tool_calls_recorded:
+        raw_call = tool_calls_recorded[0]
+        paren_idx = raw_call.find("(")
+        if paren_idx != -1:
+            first_tool_call = raw_call[:paren_idx].strip()
+            first_tool_arg = raw_call[paren_idx + 1 : -1].strip()
+
+    # Evaluasi Kategori
+    is_false_call = False
+    is_wrong_tool = False
+    is_invalid_arg = False
+    is_fidelity_ok = False
+    is_success = False
+    sandbox_blocked = False
+    reached_confirmation = False
+
+    # Deteksi status konfirmasi & sandbox
+    obs_all = " ".join(tool_observations_recorded)
+    if "[Kebijakan Ditolak]" in obs_all or "[Akses Ditolak]" in obs_all:
+        sandbox_blocked = True
+    if len(confirmed_commands_log) > 0:
+        reached_confirmation = True
+
+    if expected_tool is None:
+        # Prompt Negatif atau Prompt Memori (yang diharapkan dijawab lewat recall pasif)
+        if first_tool_call is not None:
+            is_false_call = True
+        else:
+            is_success = True
+            # Jika ini MEM, periksa fidelity kata kunci
+            if category == "MEM" and expected_key:
+                if expected_key.lower() in final_text.lower():
+                    is_fidelity_ok = True
+            else:
+                is_fidelity_ok = True
+    else:
+        # Prompt Positif Workspace
+        if first_tool_call is None:
+            is_wrong_tool = True
+        elif first_tool_call != expected_tool:
+            is_wrong_tool = True
+        else:
+            # Periksa argumen jika ada
+            if expected_arg and expected_arg.lower() not in (first_tool_arg or "").lower():
+                is_invalid_arg = True
+            else:
+                is_success = True
+                # Periksa fidelity
+                if expected_key:
+                    if expected_key.lower() in final_text.lower():
+                        is_fidelity_ok = True
+                else:
+                    is_fidelity_ok = True
+
+    return {
+        "id": prompt_item["id"],
+        "category": category,
+        "prompt_input": user_prompt,
+        "expected_tool": expected_tool,
+        "expected_arg": expected_arg,
+        "expected_key": expected_key,
+        "called_tool": first_tool_call,
+        "called_arg": first_tool_arg,
+        "passive_recall_injected": recall_injected,
+        "passive_recall_content": recall_content,
+        "tool_calls": tool_calls_recorded,
+        "tool_observations": tool_observations_recorded,
+        "final_response": final_text,
+        "confirmed_commands": list(confirmed_commands_log),
+        "evaluation": {
+            "is_success": is_success,
+            "is_false_call": is_false_call,
+            "is_wrong_tool": is_wrong_tool,
+            "is_invalid_arg": is_invalid_arg,
+            "is_fidelity_ok": is_fidelity_ok,
+            "sandbox_blocked": sandbox_blocked,
+            "reached_confirmation": reached_confirmation,
+            "is_timeout": is_timeout,
+            "timeout_reason": timeout_reason
+        },
+        "metrics": final_metrics
+    }
+
+def run_benchmark_suite(
+    suite_name: str,
+    prompts: List[Dict[str, Any]],
+    num_runs: int = 3,
+    output_jsonl: Optional[Path] = None
+):
+    """Menjalankan suite benchmark Set 4A atau Set 4R secara terisolasi."""
+    temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"enki_bench_{suite_name}_")
+    ws_path = Path(temp_dir) / "workspace"
+    data_path = Path(temp_dir) / "data"
+    log_path = Path(temp_dir) / "logs"
+
+    data_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    log_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+    os.environ["ENKI_DATA_DIR"] = str(data_path)
+    os.environ["ENKI_LOG_DIR"] = str(log_path)
+
+    setup_real_temporary_workspace(ws_path)
+
+    db_path = log_path / "sessions.db"
+    db = MemoryDatabase(db_path=db_path)
+
+    # Inisialisasi memori masa lalu untuk prompt MEM
+    for p in prompts:
+        if p.get("memory_inject"):
+            u_role, u_msg, a_role, a_msg = p["memory_inject"]
+            sess = db.create_session()
+            db.add_turn(sess, u_role, u_msg)
+            db.add_turn(sess, a_role, a_msg)
+
+    client = OllamaClient()
+    if not client.is_ready():
+        raise RuntimeError("Server Ollama tidak aktif!")
+
+    assembler = PromptAssembler(system_prompt=DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT, profile="")
+
+    all_runs_results = []
+    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
+    print(f"MEMULAI {suite_name.upper()} ({len(prompts)} PROMPT x {num_runs} RUNS):")
+    print("=" * 80)
+
+    for run_idx in range(1, num_runs + 1):
+        print(f"\n--- Menjalankan Run {run_idx}/{num_runs} ---")
+        run_results = []
+
+        for p in prompts:
+            confirmed_commands = []
+            def confirm_tracker(cmd: str) -> bool:
+                confirmed_commands.append(cmd)
+                return True  # Auto-approve untuk harness
+
+            coordinator = ReActCoordinator(
+                client=client,
+                assembler=assembler,
+                workspace_root=ws_path,
+                db=db,
+                confirm_func=confirm_tracker,
+                audit_log_path=data_path / "tool_audit.log"
+            )
+
+            sess_id = db.create_session()
+            t0 = time.time()
+            res = run_single_benchmark_turn(
+                prompt_item=p,
+                coordinator=coordinator,
+                session_id=sess_id,
+                db=db,
+                confirmed_commands_log=confirmed_commands
+            )
+            elapsed = time.time() - t0
+
+            # Catat run_idx & suite_name
+            res["run_idx"] = run_idx
+            res["benchmark_set"] = suite_name
+            run_results.append(res)
+
+            status_str = "PASS" if res["evaluation"]["is_success"] else "FAIL"
+            print(f"  [{res['id']:<14}] {status_str} ({elapsed:.2f}s) | Tool: {res['called_tool']} | Recall Inj: {res['passive_recall_injected']}")
+
+            if output_jsonl:
+                with open(output_jsonl, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                    f.write(json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
+
+        all_runs_results.append(run_results)
+
+    # Bersihkan temp
+    db.close()
+    shutil.rmtree(temp_dir, ignore_errors=True)
+    if "ENKI_DATA_DIR" in os.environ:
+        del os.environ["ENKI_DATA_DIR"]
+    if "ENKI_LOG_DIR" in os.environ:
+        del os.environ["ENKI_LOG_DIR"]
+

+
+        print("\nBENCHMARK SET 4 SELESAI. Raw JSONL tersimpan di:", out_file)
+

+        self.assertEqual(recorded_cmds, ["python --version"])

-        """Jika konfirmasi manual disetujui (True), perintah dieksekusi dan dibungkus untrusted tag."""
-        approved_confirmer = lambda cmd: True
+    def test_run_unittests_execution_and_confirmation(self):

-        res = execute_shell_command(
-            "python --version",

+
+    def test_list_dir_safe_listing_and_traversal_blocking(self):
+        """Memastikan list_dir membaca folder workspace dan memblokir path traversal."""
+        # Buat beberapa file dummy
+        (self.workspace / "file1.txt").write_text("hello", encoding="utf-8")
+        sub_dir = self.workspace / "src"
+        sub_dir.mkdir()
+        (sub_dir / "app.py").write_text("print('hi')", encoding="utf-8")
+
+        # 1. Listing root workspace
+        ok, is_policy_rejection, msg = execute_list_dir(".", self.workspace, audit_log_path=self.audit_log)
+        self.assertTrue(ok)
+        self.assertFalse(is_policy_rejection)
+        self.assertIn("[FILE] file1.txt", msg)
+        self.assertIn("[DIR]  src/", msg)
+
+        # 2. Listing sub-folder
+        ok, is_policy_rejection, msg = execute_list_dir("src", self.workspace, audit_log_path=self.audit_log)
+        self.assertTrue(ok)
+        self.assertIn("[FILE] app.py", msg)
+
+        # 3. Path traversal di luar workspace: WAJIB ditolak kebijakan
+        traversal_attempts = ["../", "../../windows", "C:\\Windows", "/etc"]
+        for t in traversal_attempts:
+            ok, is_policy_rejection, msg = execute_list_dir(t, self.workspace, audit_log_path=self.audit_log)
+            self.assertFalse(ok)
+            self.assertTrue(is_policy_rejection, f"Path traversal '{t}' harus memicu policy rejection")
+            self.assertIn("di luar direktori kerja", msg)
+
+    def test_no_arbitrary_string_command_execution_pathway(self):
+        """Memastikan tidak ada API atau parameter yang menerima string perintah bebas untuk dieksekusi."""
+        # Seluruh tool shell menerima argumen tetap (enum/void/path), tidak ada string command execution
+        import enki.tools.shell_tools as st
+        self.assertFalse(has_wrap := hasattr(st, "execute_shell_command"), "execute_shell_command seharusnya sudah dihapus")
+        self.assertTrue(hasattr(st, "execute_git_command"))
+        self.assertTrue(hasattr(st, "execute_python_version"))
+        self.assertTrue(hasattr(st, "execute_run_unittests"))
+        self.assertTrue(hasattr(st, "execute_list_dir"))
 
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     unittest.main()
